```js
const fs = await import('node:fs');
const path = 'D:/CZB/tools/ui-shots/shots.mjs';
const src = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
const lines = src.split('\n');

function findLine(pred) {
  const idx = lines.findIndex(pred);
  if (idx < 0) throw new Error('找不到锚点');
  return idx;
}
function spliceAt(idx, count, block) {
  const put = block.split('\n');
  lines.splice(idx, count, ...put);
}
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Pane body: `<div className={'min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto ' + ...}>` — lastElementChild of the section ✓ and it is the scroller. So `bottom('claims')` should work... unless there is no scrolling needed because with the new grid the body height expands to full content (since the parent grid row is `minmax(0,3fr)` but the Pane section itself might not be constrained → its height = content, so body has no overflow).

In the new interview page, middle column grid is a direct child of the outer grid; the outer grid is `grid min-h-0 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-[...]`. The outer grid's height = main's content height (main is overflow-auto, so content grows). Hence grid rows grow with content → panes never scroll → the whole `<main>` scrolls.

So earlier (pre-P15) the four-column layout probably had a fixed height via `h-full` or the main was the scroller anyway... Regardless, now the scroller is `<main>`.

So the fix for shots: use main-scrolling expressions. Define helpers:
```
const mainPane = "document.querySelector('main')";
const mainTop = mainPane + '.scrollTop = 0';
const mainBottom = mainPane + '.scrollTop = ' + mainPane + '.scrollHeight';
```
Then:
- 01-intake: mainTop (fine)
- 06-done-top: mainTop
- 07-verdicts: mainBottom
- 13-light: mainTop
- 08-resume-focus: the expr clicks a quote then sets claims pane scrollTop — change to mainBottom? Actually 08 wants the right column focus; the shot shows right pane. Keep the click and set main scroll to maybe 0. Let me read line 421-422 in current file (08-resume-focus).

Also 20-shortfall / 19-requirements / 17-adopt / 24-diff / 22-supplement / 23-counters / 10-contracts / 11-system-prompt use scrollIntoView on their element — those work with main-scroll since scrollIntoView scrolls the nearest scrollable ancestor (main) ✓.

But wait: with panes not scrolling, does the page look OK? Presumably yes (main scrolls). Fine.

Let me check the actual 06-done-top.png: it's byte-identical to 07-verdicts, and 06's expr was `top('claims')` (no-op) → both show main at scrollTop 0. The 06 shot then shows the top of the interview page. Is the top of the interview page the live/stage area or the claims? Both fine.

So: update shots to use main scroll for 06/07/01/13 and 08. Then re-run shots. Re-running takes ~2 min; acceptable.

Wait — but should I also reconsider whether this "panes don't scroll" layout is itself a UX regression from the P15 refactor? In the pre-P15 layout, `.pane` was a flex column with min-h-0 inside a fixed-height container; after the refactor to pages, the main is the scroller. Compare with the e2e passing (it doesn't check scrolling). The user's requirement was left sidebar multi-page; a single scrolling main per page is reasonable for intake/report/history/settings but for the interview page (three columns) it means the right column and middle column scroll together — acceptable but slightly worse than independent panes. Hmm — actually wait, is that true? Let me check what the interview page actually looks like: read 06-done-top.png image to see. If the whole page is one long scroll, the three columns are laid out side by side with their own heights (each column grows). Given claims has many cards, the middle column would be very tall; the right column (resume/JD) would be short. So scrolling moves everything. That's a real UX degradation vs. independent pane scrolling.

I should restore pane-independent scrolling for the interview page: make the outer grid constrained to the viewport height (h-full) so that `min-h-0` cascades and Pane bodies scroll. The main is `min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto p-3` (a flex child of the row container `flex min-h-0 flex-1`). So main has a bounded height. Inside, the interview branch wraps in `<div className="grid min-h-0 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-[...]">` — this div is a block child of main; its height is content-driven (no h-full). Adding `h-full` to that div would make it exactly main's height → panes scroll internally. But main has `overflow-auto` + `p-3`; with h-full the div = main's content box height → good.

But also for the middle column grid `grid min-h-0 gap-3 xl:grid-rows-[minmax(0,3fr)_...]` needs `h-full`? It's a grid item in the outer grid (align-items stretch by default) → its height = row height = outer grid height ✓. And each column div: `<div className="grid min-h-0 gap-3 xl:grid-rows-[...]">` is a grid item, stretched ✓. Left aside is `flex min-h-0 flex-col gap-3` stretched ✓. Right column `flex min-h-0 flex-col gap-3` stretched ✓.

So adding `h-full` (or `min-h-0 h-full`) to the interview wrapper should restore independent scrolling. That's a UI fix worth doing — and it makes the pane-scroll-based screenshots work as before (06 vs 07 differ).

But careful about main `overflow-auto`: with h-full child, the child equals main's height; main won't scroll. Good.

Also: does this break small screens? On small screens the grid is single column; h-full with content taller than main → children would overflow clipped? With `h-full` and single-column grid, the items stack; the grid height = h-full (main height) but content taller → grid rows would be `auto` sized and overflow the container... With `grid-rows` unspecified on small screens, rows are auto → the grid's own height is h-full but content overflows → since main has overflow-auto, the grid's overflow would be visible/clipped? Actually a grid with fixed height and auto rows will overflow visibly; the parent main won't scroll because the child's scrollHeight... hmm, the grid's content overflows its box, and main's scrollHeight would account for the overflowing grid? Overflow of a child is included in the scrollable overflow region of the ancestor scroller, so main would still scroll. Typically yes, main scrolls to reveal overflowing grid content.

Safer: `xl:h-full` (only apply at xl breakpoint where the three columns exist). On small screens no h-full → normal flow (pages stacked, main scrolls). Let's do that.

Let me verify current classes: line 1197: `<div className="grid min-h-0 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-[300px_minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,660px)]">` → add `xl:h-full`.

<details><summary>✅ 执行结果（原始 2459 字符，已折叠）</summary>

```text
=== ADR-0026
1|# ADR-0026：导出的 Word 是真 .docx，不是改后缀的网页
2|
3|- 状态：已采纳（2026-10-28）
4|- 相关：ADR-0006（报告导出：服务端出 HTML，PDF 交给浏览器）、ADR-0007（导入通道与依赖纪律）、ADR-0025（判定台账自写 .xlsx）、ADR-0020（单文件打包）
5|
6|## 背景
7|
8|在此之前，界面上写「导出 Word」的三颗按钮给的都是**同一份 HTML 换了个 `.doc` 后缀**（`application/msword`）。
9|Word 打得开，但打开的是网页：段落是 `div`、缩进靠 CSS、没有样式名。用户照着改两个字，
10|拿到的是「HTML 排版恰好长得像 Word」的东西；招聘方那边也容易当成可疑附件。
11|当初这么做的理由是对的（少一个依赖就少一个复现失败点，见 ADR-0006），但代价一直被记在候选清单里
12|（`docs/feature-candidates.md` 的 B3，当时的判断是「不做：提示那一下警告，比一个可能打不开的 docx 更安全」）。
13|
14|现在条件变了：判定台账已经证明「最窄的 OOXML + 自写 zip」这条路可行（ADR-0025），
15|而 .docx 与 .xlsx 共用同一个容器机制。于是把 B3 做掉，并把判断反过来：**不引依赖不等于不做真格式**。
16|
17|## 决策
18|
19|1. **三条导出路由一律出真 .docx**：`report.docx` / `resume.docx` / `tailored.docx`，
20|   MIME `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document`。
21|   旧的 `.doc` 路径不再生成（命中即 404，测试里的 404 循环已随之改）。
22|2. **自己写写入器，不引第三方库**：`packages/server/src/docx.ts` 只产出 Word 能打开的最小集合——
23|   `[Content_Types].xml` / `_rels/.rels` / `word/document.xml` / `word/_rels/document.xml.rels` / `word/styles.xml`，
```

Two issues:
1. My JSX comment insertion broke syntax: I inserted a JSX expression comment `{/* ... */}` right after `{page === 'interview' ? (` — i.e. between the ternary's `(` and the element, creating two children in a ternary branch → invalid. Need to move the comment above the `{page === 'interview' ? (` line, or put it inside as a JSX child (`<div>`...). Best: put the comment as a JS block before the ternary: `{/* 质询台三列... */}` as a sibling? Actually inside JSX children context, `{/* */}` is fine if it's a child of the parent element, but this spot is inside the ternary's true-branch expression, which must be a single expression. So I should remove the comment and put it before the line `{page === 'interview' ? (` — but is that line already inside JSX children of some parent? Yes, presumably `{/* comment */}` as a JSX child there is fine.

Let me look at lines 1190-1206 to fix precisely.

2. shots.log is the OLD run (from previous session? it shows timestamps no). Wait — the shots script ran despite build failing because of `&&`? But shots.log exists with content — that's from the earlier run (before my patch), since the script may have run then. Actually earlier the shots run happened before this session block; the log file .tmp/shots.log may be leftover, and EXIT=1 came from build failure.

So: fix the JSX comment, rebuild, rerun shots.

ADR-0027 content:
# ADR-0027：界面按任务分页，而不是把所有东西堆在一屏
- 状态：已采纳（2026-10-28）
- 相关：ADR-0003（前端独立成包 + 单端口）、ADR-0010（零安装启动器）、ADR-0028（桌面端用浏览器应用窗口）
## 背景
P3 起界面是一屏三栏/四栏：投料、过程、结论、原文全都摊在同一页上。功能长到 P12 之后，这一屏承担了太多职责——
历史会话列表挤在左栏底部、报告五种导出挤在头部、面试官工作室与模板库各占一块浮层，新手第一次打开不知道从哪下手。
用户提出「像桌面软件那样左边一列、一页一件事」。
## 决策
1. 六页：首页 / 投料台 / 质询台 / 报告 / 历史 / 设置（packages/web/src/pages.ts 的 PageId + PAGE_ITEMS + pageOf），左栏常驻导航，`#/report` 这类哈希深链可直接进页。
2. 每页只管一件事：投料台贴材料（模板、简历导入、JD 1–8 份）、质询台看这一场（阶段轨 + 结论 + 原文）、报告页只列五种带走方式、历史页只做概览与列表、设置页放配色/向导/隐私/启动。
3. 页面状态放 URL 哈希而不是路由库：只有六个页、没有嵌套路由，`location.hash` 足够；不引 react-router（每多一个依赖就多一个复现失败点）。
4. 「这一场」不跟着页面走：会话 id 仍由 useSessions 持有，切页不丢；切走再回来接着看直播。
5. 宽屏质询台仍是三列，但外层带 `xl:h-full`，三列各自滚动；少了这个约束整页会变成一个长条（这也是 06/07 两张截图逐字节相同的原因，F92）。
6. 多步向导取代「首开自动弹教程」：向导五步、走一步背景切一页，走完或跳过写 `crossexam.seen-wizard`；教程改成从按钮打开（F38 的教训：任何首开自动弹层都会打乱浏览器脚本）。
## 取舍
- 不做单页内的标签页切换（Tab）：那只是把一屏塞进去的东西换个摆法，导航栏仍然是「一页一件事」。
- 不用 react-router：见决策 3。
- 代价是「一屏看全」变成「多页来回切」：因此每一步向导都把背景切到对应页，用户不用自己记「报告在哪一页」。
- 截图与点测脚本全部按分页重写（30 张图、177 项断言），旧的「一屏塞六件事」断言必然超时——测试与界面同源，这一条成本是必须付的。

ADR-0028:
# ADR-0028：桌面端是「没有地址栏的浏览器窗口」，不是 exe
- 状态：已采纳（2026-10-28）
- 相关：ADR-0010（零安装启动器）、ADR-0003（单端口）、ADR-0027（界面按任务分页）
## 背景
用户要求「打包成桌面端，双击就能用」。参考物是他桌面上另一款软件（简历通）。
## 决策
1. 不打包 exe：Electron/Tauri 都要引入需本地编译/大体积下载的依赖，直接违反 AGENTS.md §6（十分钟复现）与 ADR-0010 的结论。
2. 启动器（tools/launcher/launch.mjs）在起完服务后找系统已有的 Edge/Chrome，用 `--app=<url> --window-size=1440,960` 开一个**没有地址栏的应用窗口**；找不到就退回系统默认浏览器（会带地址栏），并在日志里说清楚。
3. 探测顺序是纯函数：tools/launcher/src/plan.mjs 的 appBrowserCandidates / chooseAppBrowser / browserOpenArgs，按平台给候选路径，win32 依次 ProgramFiles(x86) → ProgramFiles → LOCALAPPDATA，环境变量缺失就不编路径；不传 appPath 时 browserOpenArgs 与以前逐字一致（12 → 17 条单测看守）。
4. 快捷方式仍指向 启动.cmd（ADR-0010 的形态），`--shortcut` 建桌面快捷方式那条路不变。
## 取舍
- 窗口尺寸不保证：Edge 会记住同一个 app 窗口上次的尺寸（实测请求 1440x960 开出来 945x1012），这是浏览器行为，不改。
- 「桌面端」的证据是 UIA 树里 OmniboxViewViews 的 boundingBox 为 null（地址栏确实没显示），而不是「窗口看起来像应用」。
- 代价是依赖机器上已有 Chromium 系浏览器：没有就退回带地址栏的默认浏览器，功能不缺失。

Also need to check ADR numbering/format: files named 00NN-kebab.md. So filenames: 0027-pages-by-task.md and 0028-app-window-desktop.md.

Let me write both files with the placeholder trick.

</details>

## 这一轮做了什么

**界面按任务分页**（[ADR-0027](docs/adr/0027-pages-by-task.md)）：首页 / 投料台 / 质询台 / 报告 / 历史 / 设置，每页只做一件事，`#/report` 这类哈希深链可直达但**不引路由库**；首开弹的是五步向导（欢迎 → 材料 → 阵容 → 开跑 → 带结论走），走一步背景页面跟着换，教程改成按钮打开。

**「桌面端」= 没有地址栏的应用窗口**（[ADR-0028](docs/adr/0028-app-window-desktop.md)）：启动器在 Edge / Chrome / Brave 的惯常位置里挑一个存在的，用 `--app=<url> --window-size=1440,960` 打开；找不到就退回系统默认浏览器（会带地址栏），不会变成打不开。真机验证过：UIA 里 `OmniboxViewViews` 的 boundingBox 为 `null`，地址栏确实不在。**没有打包 exe**——那会引入需要本地编译的原生依赖，违反 [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) §6，也会毁掉十分钟复现。

## 顺带抓到的两个真缺陷

1. `docs/screenshots/06-done-top.png` 与 `07-verdicts.png` **逐字节相同**（SHA256 都是 `1937deac…`）。根因是分页后质询台三列外层没有高度约束，三栏各自长到内容那么高，真正滚动的是外层 `<main>`，`Pane` 的正文容器 `scrollHeight === clientHeight`，「滚到顶截图」成了空操作。修法是一行 `xl:h-full`（[App.tsx](packages/web/src/App.tsx)）。记进 [findings.md](findings.md) F92：两张截图哈希相同，值得当失败看。
2. 清空历史是**乐观删除**，点测原来等「DOM 少一行」，最后一次点击还没落地循环就退出了 → 改成同时等服务端会话数下降。

## 门禁（2026-10-28 权威值）

| 门禁 | 结果 |
| --- | --- |
| `npm run verify` | ✅ 73 个测试文件 / **765 条断言** + 9 项冒烟 + 构建 + 14 项性能预算 |
| `npm run e2e:ui` | ✅ **177/177**，控制台 0 报错（分页重写前 165） |
| `npm run shots` | ✅ **30 张**截图、控制台 0 问题 |
| `npm run export:prompt` | ✅ **59 份快照**（52 份分片，23.5 MB），脱敏命中 5 类 |
| `npm run docs:pdf` | ✅ **30 页** / 2128 KB（上限 30，**余量为 0**，已写进 [roadmap-v2.md](docs/roadmap-v2.md)） |
| `npm run repro` | ✅ 干净目录 **86.6 s**（40.1 + 16.0 + 25.7 + 0.8），6.9 倍余量 |

文档全部同步完毕：技术文档三章 + 三份视频脚本 + 截图清单 + 发布清单，共 8 个小提交（含重新导出的 Prompt 链）。

Hmm, Tailwind v4 `@theme` sets design tokens that generate utilities (e.g. `--color-sky-500` → `bg-sky-500`). But Tailwind's default palette already has sky-500 etc.; overriding is fine and gives us consistent brand colors in both themes... careful: we want light/dark variants for these too. Since components use them via classes like `text-sky-400`, and we override the variables in light theme, they'll adapt.

Wait: Tailwind class generation uses the @theme value at build; using CSS var references is not how @theme works for arbitrary var override at runtime? Actually Tailwind v4 emits utilities like `.bg-sky-500 { background-color: var(--color-sky-500) }`? In v4, theme values become CSS variables in :root and utilities reference them (yes, v4 emits `--color-sky-500` in :root and utilities use `var(--color-sky-500)` — that's why our light-theme override of --color-ink-* works). Good, existing code relies on that.

But careful: overriding --color-sky-500 in `:root` after Tailwind emits its :root block — our `@theme` block declares the dark values, and `:root[data-theme='light']` overrides. That's the existing pattern. Good.

Design: 
```tsx
import type { CSSProperties } from 'react';

export type IconName = 'home' | 'intake' | ...;

const PATHS: Record<IconName, string[]> = { ... };

export function Icon(props: { name: IconName; size?: number; className?: string; strokeWidth?: number }) {
  const d = PATHS[props.name];
  const size = props.size ?? 16;
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={props.strokeWidth ?? 1.7} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" className={'shrink-0 ' + (props.className ?? '')} aria-hidden="true">
      {d.map((p, i) => <path key={i} d={p} />)}
    </svg>
  );
}
```
Some icons need circles/rects; I can express circle as path arcs: `M12 3a9 9 0 1 1 0 18 9 9 0 0 1 0-18` etc. Keep all as paths — simpler.
